function globeProject(lon,lat,w,h){const canvas=document.getElementById('globe-canvas');w=w||canvas?._cssW||360;h=h||canvas?._cssH||w;const rad=Math.PI/180,l=(lon-globeLon)*rad,p=lat*rad,p0=globeLat*rad,cp=Math.cos(p),sp=Math.sin(p),c0=Math.cos(p0),s0=Math.sin(p0),cosc=s0*sp+c0*cp*Math.cos(l);if(cosc<=0)return null;const r=Math.min(w,h)*.455*globeZoom;return [w/2+r*cp*Math.sin(l),h/2-r*(c0*sp-s0*cp*Math.cos(l)),cosc]} function drawProjectedLine(ctx,coords,w,h){let started=false,last=null;const always starts on Home. // - Short background pauses keep the current screen. // - After 10 minutes away, return to Home while preserving any unfinished session. const APP_RESUME_HOME_AFTER_MS=10*60*1000; let appHiddenAt=null; function returnHomeAfterLongPause(){ if(activeScreenName()==='session'&&state.activeSession)persistActiveSession(); setTab('home'); renderHomeContinue(); } document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{ if(document.visibilityState==='hidden'){ appHiddenAt=Date.now(); if(activeScreenName()==='session'&&state.activeSession)persistActiveSession(); return; } if(document.visibilityState==='visible'&&appHiddenAt){ const awayFor=Date.now()-appHiddenAt; appHiddenAt=null; if(awayFor>=APP_RESUME_HOME_AFTER_MS)returnHomeAfterLongPause(); } }); window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{ appHiddenAt=Date.now(); if(activeScreenName()==='session'&&state.activeSession)persistActiveSession(); }); window.addEventListener('pageshow',event=>{ if(!event.persisted)return; const awayFor=appHiddenAt?Date.now()-appHiddenAt:APP_RESUME_HOME_AFTER_MS; appHiddenAt=null; if(awayFor>=APP_RESUME_HOME_AFTER_MS)returnHomeAfterLongPause(); }); // Initial render / recovery renderAll();updateOnboarding(); // norwegian-content.js is deferred. Refresh journey-derived UI after deferred local content has executed. document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{ renderAll(); if(activeScreenName()==='subject'&&activeSubject){renderSubjectModules();renderSubjectJourney();renderSubjectContinue()} if(activeScreenName()==='geography')renderGeographyContinue(); }); const needsProfileSetup=Number(state.profile.setupVersion||0)<1||!String(state.profile.name||'').trim()||(state.profile.avatar!=='boy'&&state.profile.avatar!=='girl'); if(needsProfileSetup||!state.profile.onboarded||!state.profile.grade){ onboardingStep=needsProfileSetup?0:(state.profile.onboarded?2:0); selectedGrade=Number(state.profile.grade)||null; profileName=typeof state.profile.name==='string'?state.profile.name.trim().slice(0,20):''; selectedAvatar=state.profile.avatar==='girl'?'girl':state.profile.avatar==='boy'?'boy':null; if(profileNameInput)profileNameInput.value=profileName; renderAvatarChoice();renderGradeChoice();updateOnboarding();document.getElementById('onboarding').classList.add('show'); }else{ // Never drop the child straight back into yesterday's question on a fresh launch. // The unfinished session remains in state and is offered from Home. setTab('home'); renderHomeContinue(); }
